intdb {
	bel_class SLICE {
		input A0;
		input B0;
		input C0;
		input D0;
		input A1;
		input B1;
		input C1;
		input D1;
		input M0;
		input M1;
		input FXA;
		input FXB;
		input CLK;
		input LSR;
		input CE;
		input WCK;
		input WRE;
		output F0;
		output F1;
		output Q0;
		output Q1;
		output OFX0;
		output OFX1;
	}

	bel_class CIBTEST_SEL {
		input TSEL0;
		input TSEL1;
	}

	bel_class ECLK_TAP {
		output ECLK0;
		output ECLK1;
	}

	bel_class HSDCLK_ROOT {
		output OUT_W[8];
		output OUT_E[8];
	}

	region_slot PCLK;
	region_slot RSCLK[0];
	region_slot RSCLK[1];
	region_slot RSCLK[2];
	region_slot RSCLK[3];
	region_slot RSCLK_V;
	wire TIE0: tie 0;
	wire TIE1: tie 1;
	wire X0_W[0]: mux;
	wire X0_W[1]: mux;
	wire X0_E[0]: mux;
	wire X0_E[1]: mux;
	wire X0_S[0]: mux;
	wire X0_S[1]: mux;

				output UFMFAIL = CELL.OUT_Q[0];
			}

				input USRGOE = CELL.IMUX_C[0];
			}
